What's on in Cambridge — 8 Oct 2026editor's version

Events 8 Oct – 18 Oct · weekends: 10–11 Oct and 17–18 Oct

This week Cambridge remembers its own psychedelic son — Syd Barrett would have turned 80 this year, and the Corn Exchange marks it with a tribute concert in the very hall where he played his last gig. Elsewhere, Nish Kumar on Saturday and The Beat on Sunday of the second weekend, Alice Cooper talks through his new memoir, and UNIQLO's first Cambridge store has just opened its doors.

Theme of the week: Syd Barrett at 80

Cambridge's most famous psychedelic son, Syd Barrett, founder of Pink Floyd, would have turned 80 this year — he was born in the city on 6 January 1946 and died here in 2006. This week the Corn Exchange, where he gave his very last concert in 1972, hosts a tribute, alongside a small gallery show, a director's talk with a screening, and even an unexpected football connection.

A Tribute to Syd Barrett – Official 80th Anniversary Celebrations

Sat 10 Oct · Cambridge Corn Exchange, Cambridge · £55–£132

The night's centrepiece: a concert of Barrett's songs in the hall where he played his very last gig, in 1972. Kula Shaker and Soft Machine are among those performing his music, with a light show from The Mad Alchemist; proceeds go to mental health charities.

More →

Have You Got It Yet? The Story of Syd Barrett and Pink Floyd: director's talk and screening

Fri 9 Oct, 17:00 and 20:30 · Openspace / Arts Picturehouse, Cambridge · £10 (talk); prices on the website (screening)

Director Roddy Bogawa talks about making his 2023 documentary on Syd Barrett and Pink Floyd and shows unseen outtakes at 5pm at Openspace; the film itself screens at the Arts Picturehouse at 8.30pm the same evening.

More →

Syd Barrett Exhibition: Life, Art and Cultural Impact

until 11 Oct · Openspace, Cambridge · prices on the website

A small show of original paintings, sketches and new work by contemporary artists inspired by Barrett's art and music.

More →

Cambridge United v Blackpool

Sat 10 Oct, 15:00 · Abbey Stadium, Cambridge · prices on the website

An otherwise ordinary League One fixture gains a Barrett connection: his sister Rosemary Breen has said Cambridge United are donating proceeds from this match to mental health charities — a nod to World Mental Health Day, which falls the same weekend.

More →

The weekend: 10–11 Oct

Roachford + Hue and Cry

Sun 11 Oct · Cambridge Corn Exchange, Cambridge · £38–£113

Corn Exchange is hosting a proper double headline night: Roachford, the voice behind soul hit 'Cuddly Toy', sharing the bill with Scottish duo Hue and Cry, who gave us 'Labour of Love'. Both acts play full sets with full bands, so it's two 80s catalogues for the price of one ticket.

More →

Spem In Alium: I Fagiolini at 40

Sun 11 Oct, 15:30 · Saffron Hall, Saffron Walden · from £20

Vocal ensemble I Fagiolini mark their 40th anniversary with Thomas Tallis's extraordinary 40-voice motet Spem in Alium, sung from all round the hall. Also on the bill: Jess Gillam and her Band.

More →

Ely’s Autumn and Orchard Fayre 2026

10–11 Oct · Ely · prices on the website

Ely's autumn fair: local food, drink and crafts, apple-themed activities and live music over two days in October.

Arbury Social

Opened 25 Sep · Meadows Community Centre, Arbury Road, Cambridge

A new not-for-profit community café has opened at the Meadows Community Centre, run by Cambridge Sustainable Food CIC with Cambridge City Council. It serves meals made from scratch using seasonal, surplus and locally sourced ingredients, aimed at cutting food waste and keeping prices affordable.
